It is collected inside leg 0's `placeOrderFor`, so it lands in the builder's SpotPool vault balance for that leg's input token, withdrawable exactly like any other builder fee (see [Builder Fees](/developers/http-api/builder-fees)). Attribution itself is swap-level: one [`SwapAttributed`](#swapattributed) event covers the whole route.

```solidity
function swapExactInWithBuilder(SwapExactInParams calldata params, address builder, uint96 builderFeeBpsTimes1k)
    external payable
    returns (uint256 amountOut, uint256 amountInUsed);

function swapExactOutWithBuilder(SwapExactOutParams calldata params, address builder, uint96 builderFeeBpsTimes1k)
    external payable
    returns (uint256 amountIn, uint256 amountOutReceived);

function quoteExactInWithBuilder(SwapLeg[] calldata route, address inputToken, uint256 inputAmount, uint96 builderFeeBpsTimes1k)
    external view returns (QuoteResult memory result);

function quoteExactOutWithBuilder(SwapLeg[] calldata route, address inputToken, address outputToken, uint256 outputAmount, uint96 builderFeeBpsTimes1k)
    external view returns (QuoteResult memory result);

function quoteMarketExactInWithBuilder(SwapLeg[] calldata route, address inputToken, uint256 inputAmount, uint96 builderFeeBpsTimes1k)
    external view returns (QuoteResult memory result);
```

**Quote with the same rate you intend to swap with.** The builder fee comes out of leg 0's principal budget, so a quote taken at rate `0` overstates what a swap at rate `r` will deliver. Passing `builderFeeBpsTimes1k = 0` to any `…WithBuilder` quote reproduces the plain quote exactly.

**Prerequisites**, all enforced at swap time against the *live* pool state:

* The user must have called [`approveBuilder`](/developers/contracts/functions#approvebuilder) on the **leg-0 pool** for this builder, for at least `builderFeeBpsTimes1k`. Read the effective ceiling with [`getEffectiveBuilderApproval`](/developers/contracts/functions#geteffectivebuilderapproval), which already folds in the pool cap.
* The leg-0 pool's `getMaxBuilderFeeBpsTimes1k()` must be ≥ the rate. Every mainnet and testnet pool currently runs a `100000` (100 BPS) cap.
* The builder may not be the router itself, nor any pool on the route.

***

### `quoteExactOutRequiredInput`

Returns the quote **and** the exact `maxInputAmount` to pass to the matching `swapExactOut`. Use this rather than padding a quote with headroom.

```solidity
function quoteExactOutRequiredInput(
    SwapLeg[] calldata route,
    address inputToken,
    address outputToken,
    uint256 outputAmount,
    uint96 builderFeeBpsTimes1k
) external view returns (QuoteResult memory result, uint256 requiredMaxInputAmount);
```

`requiredMaxInputAmount` is both **necessary and sufficient**: passing it verbatim can neither revert `InsufficientLegInput` at leg 0 nor `ExcessiveInput` at the end.

{% hint style="warning" %}
**For native input this is the only figure that works — not merely a safe upper bound.** Exact-out with native input requires `maxInputAmount == msg.value`, *and* `msg.value` to equal leg 0's native requirement exactly, because the pool's auto-pull enforces strict equality. Padded headroom **reverts** where this value succeeds.
{% endhint %}

`requiredMaxInputAmount` is `0` whenever `result.ok == false` — there is no executable swap to size, and you must not submit one. Pass `builderFeeBpsTimes1k = 0` to size a plain `swapExactOut`.

***

### `maxLegs`

```solidity
function maxLegs() external view returns (uint256);
```

Returns the cap on `route.length`. Default is `8`.

***

### `getSpotPoolRegistry`

```solidity
function getSpotPoolRegistry() external view returns (ISpotPoolRegistry);
```

Returns the current `SpotPoolRegistry` pointer the router consults for the per-leg `isRegistered` check.

## Events

### `SwapExecuted`

Emitted once per successful swap, after all legs have settled.

```solidity
event SwapExecuted(
    address indexed caller,
    address indexed inputToken,
    address indexed outputToken,
    uint256 amountIn,
    uint256 amountOut
);
```

| Parameter     | Type      | Description                                                         |
| ------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `caller`      | `address` | Order owner (`msg.sender` to the router, the auto-deliver target).  |
| `inputToken`  | `address` | Input token (ERC-20 address or `NATIVE_TOKEN`).                     |
| `outputToken` | `address` | Final leg's output token.                                           |
| `amountIn`    | `uint256` | Caller's wallet input delta. For native input, exactly `msg.value`. |
| `amountOut`   | `uint256` | Caller's wallet output delta.                                       |

### `SwapAttributed`

Emitted once per builder-attributed swap, alongside [`SwapExecuted`](#swapexecuted). Attribution is **swap-level, not leg-level** — one event covers the whole route regardless of how many legs it had. Plain (unattributed) swaps do not emit it, and `SwapExecuted`'s own signature is unchanged, so existing indexers are unaffected.

```solidity
event SwapAttributed(address indexed caller, address indexed builder, uint96 builderFeeBpsTimes1k);
```

**topic0:** `0x2abe99d2513ecc9757b8244d99f4d831157b1b03dea4e046500a77cefbc21eff`

| Parameter              | Type      | Description                                     |
| ---------------------- | --------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| `caller`               | `address` | The swapping user (indexed).                    |
| `builder`              | `address` | Builder credited with the swap (indexed).       |
| `builderFeeBpsTimes1k` | `uint96`  | Rate applied to leg 0, in BPS\_TIMES\_1K units. |

The fee *amount* is not in this event — it is in the leg-0 pool's [`BuilderFeeCharged`](/developers/contracts/events#builderfeecharged), which names the token and the exact figure.

## Errors

The router decodes a fine-grained error surface so UIs can map specific reverts to user-actionable copy.

| Error                                                              | When                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `RouteEmpty()`                                                     | `route.length == 0`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `RouteTooLong(maxLegs)`                                            | `route.length > maxLegs()`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `DeadlineExpired(deadline, currentTs)`                             | `block.timestamp * 1e9 > deadlineNs`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `TokenZero()`                                                      | Input or output token is the zero address.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `InputEqualsOutput()`                                              | `inputToken == outputToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `InvalidMsgValue(expected, actual)`                                | `msg.value` mismatch on entry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `RouteTokenMismatch(legIndex, expected, base, quote)`              | Running token not in the leg's `(base, quote)`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `FinalTokenMismatch(expected, actual)`                             | Last leg's output ≠ `outputToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `RouterInvalidLegPrice(legIndex, priceLimit, tickSize)`            | `priceLimit` zero or unaligned (on swap entries — not market quotes).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PoolNotRegistered(legIndex, pool)`                                | Pool not in the linked `SpotPoolRegistry`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `InvalidLegQuantity(legIndex)`                                     | `quantity != 0` on `swapExactIn` / `quoteMarketExactIn` / `quoteExactIn`; or `quantity == 0` on `swapExactOut`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `RouterQuantityBelowMinimum(legIndex, qty, minQty)`                | Leg quantity below pool's `minQuantity`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `RouterQuantityNotLotAligned(legIndex, qty, lotSize)`              | Exact-out leg quantity not lot-aligned.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `InsufficientLegInput(legIndex, available, required)`              | Running input < leg deposit.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `LegPlacementRejected(legIndex, reason)`                           | The pool refused leg `legIndex`. `reason` is the pool's raw revert data — decode its first 4 bytes against [SpotPool errors](/developers/contracts/errors#order-rejection). In practice: `ImmediateOrCancelNoFill` (exact-in leg filled nothing), `FillOrKillNotFillable` (exact-out leg unfillable), or `SelfMatchCancelTaker` (you are the resting maker on the other side). |
| `LegPlacementRevertedWithoutReason(legIndex)`                      | Leg `legIndex` reverted with **empty** returndata — a child-frame out-of-gas, not a decision by the pool. Raise the gas limit; do not treat it as "no liquidity".                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `LegFillFailed(legIndex)`                                          | Legacy path: inner `placeOrderFor` returned `success = false`. Only reachable against a pre-upgrade pool; an upgraded pool reverts and surfaces as `LegPlacementRejected` instead.                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `InsufficientOutput(received, minRequired)`                        | Total output delta < `minOutputAmount`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ExcessiveInput(spent, maxAllowed)`                                | Exact-out: total input spend > `maxInputAmount`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NativeIntermediateUnsupported(legIndex)`                          | A non-final leg outputs `NATIVE_TOKEN`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `RouterNotApprovedAsOperator(legIndex, pool)`                      | Caller has not granted the router operator approval.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `RouterQuoteInputZero()`                                           | Quote called with `inputAmount == 0`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RouterQuoteOutputZero()`                                          | Quote called with `outputAmount == 0`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `RouterMarketQuoteInvalidPriceLimit(legIndex, providedPriceLimit)` | `quoteMarketExactIn` called with `priceLimit != 0` on any leg.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NativeTransferFailed()` / `NativeRefundFailed()`                  | Native push to the caller failed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `InsufficientGasForPayout(gasLeft)`                                | Native-base BUY broadcast with too low a gas limit (the inner SpotPool's payout-path guard tripped). Set ≥ 5,000,000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RouterBuilderFeeWithoutBuilder(rate)`                             | Non-zero `builderFeeBpsTimes1k` with `builder == address(0)`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `RouterBuilderCodesNotSupportedOnPool(legIndex, pool)`             | The leg-0 pool's builder cap is `0`, so it cannot charge a builder fee.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `RouterBuilderFeeExceedsPoolCap(...)`                              | Rate exceeds the leg-0 pool's `getMaxBuilderFeeBpsTimes1k()`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `RouterBuilderNotApproved(legIndex, pool, rate, approved)`         | User has not [`approveBuilder`](/developers/contracts/functions#approvebuilder)'d this builder on the leg-0 pool for at least `rate`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RouterBuilderIsPool(legIndex, pool)`                              | The builder address is one of the route's pools.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `RouterBuilderIsRouter()`                                          | The builder address is the router itself.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

### Recommended UX copy mapping

| Solidity error                              | UX copy                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `LegPlacementRejected` / `LegFillFailed`    | "No liquidity at your price — try a smaller amount or widen slippage."               |
| ↳ inner `SelfMatchCancelTaker`              | "You're already resting an order on the other side of this market. Cancel it first." |
| `LegPlacementRevertedWithoutReason`         | "The swap ran out of gas. Try again with a higher gas limit."                        |
| `InsufficientOutput`                        | "Price moved more than your slippage tolerance. Try again or widen tolerance."       |
| `ExcessiveInput`                            | "Input usage exceeded your max. Try again."                                          |
| `DeadlineExpired`                           | "Order expired before submission. Try again."                                        |
| `RouterNotApprovedAsOperator`               | "Router approval missing — click Approve Router and try again."                      |
| `NativeIntermediateUnsupported`             | "Native (SOMI) cannot be used as an intermediate token."                             |
| `PoolNotRegistered`                         | "Pool is not registered with the router. Contact admin."                             |
| `FinalTokenMismatch` / `RouteTokenMismatch` | "Route doesn't link up — internal."                                                  |
| `RouterQuantityBelowMinimum`                | "Amount below the pool's minimum trade size."                                        |

## Quote → swap recipe

The router's quote returns the worst-case fill price the trade would touch. The frontend sizes the live `priceLimit` from that value, inflated by the slippage cushion, so the executed swap delivers the quoted output modulo wei-level rounding.

```solidity
// 1. Build the quote route — zeros everywhere.
ISpotRouter.SwapLeg[] memory route = new ISpotRouter.SwapLeg[](1);
route[0] = ISpotRouter.SwapLeg({
    pool: WETH_USDSO_POOL,
    priceLimit: 0,
    quantity:   0
});

// 2. Quote.
ISpotRouter.QuoteResult memory q = router.quoteMarketExactIn(route, WETH, 1e18);
require(q.ok, "Insufficient liquidity at this depth");

// 3. Build the swap route — use the quote's worstFillPrice inflated by slippage.
uint256 slippageBps = 50; // 0.5%
ISpotRouter.SwapLeg[] memory liveRoute = new ISpotRouter.SwapLeg[](route.length);
for (uint256 i = 0; i < route.length; i++) {
    liveRoute[i] = ISpotRouter.SwapLeg({
        pool: route[i].pool,
        priceLimit: tickAlignAwayFromUser(q.legs[i].worstFillPrice, slippageBps, isBid[i], tick[i]),
        quantity: 0
    });
}

// 4. Submit.
ISpotRouter.SwapExactInParams memory params = ISpotRouter.SwapExactInParams({
    inputToken:      WETH,
    inputAmount:     1e18,
    outputToken:     USDso,
    minOutputAmount: q.amountOut * (10_000 - slippageBps) / 10_000,
    route:           liveRoute,
    deadlineNs:      uint64((block.timestamp + 30 minutes) * 1e9)
});
(uint256 amountOut, uint256 amountInUsed) = router.swapExactIn(params);
```

`tickAlignAwayFromUser` rounds the inflated price away from the user's interest — up for a bid leg (user accepts paying more) and down for an ask leg (user accepts receiving less) — then snaps to the pool's `tickSize`.

For native input, set `inputToken = NATIVE_TOKEN` and attach `value = inputAmount` on the call.

## Granting operator approval

A one-time per-wallet call on the `OperatorPermissionsRegistry`. Covers every official pool the linked `SpotPoolRegistry` will ever list.

Using `cast`:

```bash
export ROUTER=0x780672aDA90Ed7cf2C3E8B70DBa87A19d584c8B0          # mainnet
export OP_REGISTRY=0xE7a190736B6024a4DbafadC04E283075877005ce     # mainnet
export PLACE_ORDER_FOR_SELECTOR=0x80054449   # bytes4(keccak256("placeOrderFor(address,bool,uint64,uint256,uint256,uint64,uint8,uint8,address,uint96)"))

cast send $OP_REGISTRY \
  "setOperatorApprovalGlobal(address,bytes4[],bool)" \
  $ROUTER "[$PLACE_ORDER_FOR_SELECTOR]" true \
  --rpc-url https://api.infra.mainnet.somnia.network/ \
  --private-key $PRIVATE_KEY
```

Verify the grant landed:

```bash
cast call $OP_REGISTRY \
  "isGloballyApproved(address,address,bytes4)(bool)" \
  $YOUR_WALLET $ROUTER $PLACE_ORDER_FOR_SELECTOR \
  --rpc-url https://api.infra.mainnet.somnia.network/
```

For pools that are NOT in the registry (rare — every official pool is registered), use `setOperatorApprovalForPool(pool, operator, selectors, approved)` instead.

## SpotPoolRegistry

The router's per-leg `isRegistered(pool)` check delegates to the `SpotPoolRegistry` (mainnet `0xB601bc1099B040E4882089D94690F7C38AF4CCD2`, testnet `0x07A29A0A086Bc8262a9320db93E603eE13D57962`). The registry is the canonical allowlist of router-routable pools — the same registry is consulted by `OperatorPermissionsRegistry` when resolving global operator approvals, so a single registration covers both the router-side legitimacy check AND the user's `setOperatorApprovalGlobal` grant.

Read functions integrators use:

```solidity
function isRegistered(address pool) external view returns (bool);
function areRegistered(address[] calldata pools) external view returns (bool[] memory);
event PoolRegistered(address indexed pool);
event PoolUnregistered(address indexed pool);
```

Owner-curated. Pools are added / removed via `registerPool` / `unregisterPool`.

## Tooling

* [**`dex-spot-router-interaction` Claude skill**](https://github.com/somnia-chain/somnia-skills) — self-contained reference (ABIs, interfaces, error-to-UX map) that any agent can load to integrate with the router without cloning the protocol repo.
* **Simple Swap** — the consumer-facing one-click swap UI built on the router; see [Simple Swap](/trading/readme-1/simple-swap) for the quote → swap flow.

## Native token sentinel

`NATIVE_TOKEN = 0x28f34DeFd2b4CB48d9eE6d89f2Be4Bc601694c00`. Use this address in `SwapLeg.pool`-adjacent fields (`inputToken`, `outputToken`, `SwapExactInParams.inputToken`, etc.) when the side is native SOMI. Calling `IERC20` on the sentinel reverts — it is a marker, not a deployed contract.

Native may only appear as **leg-0 input** or **final-leg output**. The router rejects native as the output of an intermediate leg with `NativeIntermediateUnsupported(legIndex)`.


# HTTP API

The HTTP API for Somnia DEX.

The DEX HTTP API provides a programmatic REST interface that allows integrators to list the currently-available markets and currencies, prepare orders for transmission, and receive real-time updates about all orders placed on Somnia.

See also the [WebSocket API](/developers/websocket-api) for truly real-time communication with the DEX.

Note that, unlike some DEX services, the HTTP API is not sufficient to place an order - the client is expected to be able to connect to the Somnia network and transmit their order themselves. See the [Trading](/developers/http-api/trading#post-v0-markets-symbol-orders) method for more information.

## Base URLs

Every REST endpoint is served under the `/v0` path prefix. Pick the row for your target environment and prepend the base URL to the operation paths shown throughout these docs (e.g. `GET /v0/markets` becomes `https://api.dreamdex.io/v0/markets`).

| Environment              | Chain ID | REST base URL                    | WebSocket public feed                    |
| ------------------------ | -------- | -------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------- |
| Mainnet (Somnia)         | `5031`   | `https://api.dreamdex.io/v0`     | `wss://api.dreamdex.io/v0/ws/public`     |
| Testnet (Somnia Shannon) | `50312`  | `https://stg.api.dreamdex.io/v0` | `wss://stg.api.dreamdex.io/v0/ws/public` |

{% hint style="warning" %}
The `/v0` segment is required on both environments. Omitting it (e.g. `https://stg.api.dreamdex.io/markets`) returns a 404.
{% endhint %}

The examples in these docs use the mainnet base URL. To target testnet, substitute the staging base URL above (and the [testnet contract addresses](/developers/contracts/contract-specifications#testnet-somnia-shannon-chain-id-50312)). The [Quick Start](/developers/quick-start) parameterises this as a `$BASE_URL` shell variable so a single set of commands works against either environment.

For the live markets and currency codes accepted by `{symbol}` path parameters, see [Markets and currency codes](/developers/http-api/market-data#markets-and-currency-codes).


# Error Handling

All error responses use a consistent JSON format:

```json
{
  "status": 400,
  "name": "invalid_amount",
  "description": "invalid amount: strconv.ParseFloat: parsing \"abc\": invalid syntax"
}
```

| Field         | Type    | Description                                                                            |
| ------------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `status`      | integer | HTTP status code                                                                       |
| `name`        | string  | Machine-readable error name (see table below)                                          |
| `description` | string  | Human-readable explanation (may include internal details; do not display to end users) |

The `name` field is stable and intended for programmatic error handling. The `description` field may change between releases and is intended for debugging.

## Error Names

### Validation (400)

| Name                   | Description                                                                                                       |
| ---------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `missing_field`        | Required parameter or header is missing                                                                           |
| `invalid_param`        | Parameter has an invalid format or value                                                                          |
| `invalid_request_body` | JSON body could not be parsed or fails schema validation                                                          |
| `invalid_amount`       | Amount is not a valid decimal or violates the market's lot size                                                   |
| `invalid_price`        | Price is not a valid decimal or violates the market's tick size                                                   |
| `invalid_currency`     | Currency code is not part of the market pair                                                                      |
| `invalid_order`        | Order body could not be parsed                                                                                    |
| `invalid_order_type`   | Unknown or unsupported order type, or an order type not permitted for the chosen `fundingSource` (see note below) |
| `invalid_order_id`     | Order ID is not a valid format                                                                                    |
| `invalid_fund_source`  | Funding source is incompatible with the chosen order type (see note below)                                        |
| `invalid_interval`     | Unsupported candle interval                                                                                       |
| `invalid_message`      | SIWE message could not be parsed                                                                                  |

### Authentication (401 / 403)

| Name                 | Description                           |
| -------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| `unauthorized`       | Missing or invalid bearer token       |
| `invalid_nonce`      | SIWE nonce is invalid or expired      |
| `invalid_signature`  | SIWE signature verification failed    |
| `domain_mismatch`    | SIWE domain does not match the server |
| `wallet_not_allowed` | Wallet is not authorized (403)        |

### Not Found (404)

| Name               | Description                  |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------- |
| `market_not_found` | Market symbol does not exist |
| `order_not_found`  | Order ID does not exist      |

### Server (500 / 503)

| Name              | Description                          |
| ----------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `internal_error`  | Unexpected server error              |
| `rpc_unavailable` | Blockchain node is unreachable (503) |
| `not_implemented` | Endpoint is not yet implemented      |

{% hint style="info" %}
The `Error-Name` response header also contains the error name for quick inspection without parsing the body.
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="warning" %}
**Funding source and order type must be compatible.** `invalid_fund_source` / `invalid_order_type` mean the `fundingSource` + `orderType` combination on your [prepare-order](/developers/http-api/trading#post-v0-markets-symbol-orders) request is not accepted for that path. Resting maker types (GTC, PostOnly) settle through the vault / auto-pull `placeOrder` path. See the [funding source to function map](/developers/contracts/functions#funding-source-to-function-map).
{% endhint %}


# Authentication

The DEX uses [Sign-In with Ethereum (ERC-4361)](https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-4361) for authentication. Authentication is required for private endpoints such as [preparing orders](/developers/http-api/trading#post-v0-markets-symbol-orders) and [vault operations](/developers/http-api/vault); public [market data](/developers/http-api/market-data) endpoints do not require it.

To authenticate, first request a nonce, include it in a SIWE message signed by your wallet, then submit the signed message to receive a JWT bearer token.

The `Chain ID` field in the SIWE message identifies the target network and must match the environment you are trading on: `5031` for Somnia mainnet, `50312` for Somnia Shannon testnet. It is the same chain ID carried in the `chainId` field of the unsigned transactions the API returns, so the login message and the orders you broadcast are signed against the same network.

## Token lifetime and refresh

The JWT is valid for **1 hour**. The [login response](#post-v0-auth-login) includes an `expiresAt` field (Unix milliseconds) - the exact expiry; do not hard-code the duration. There is **no refresh endpoint**: to stay authenticated you re-run the full nonce → sign → login flow to obtain a fresh token. Once expired, requests to private endpoints return [`unauthorized` (401)](/developers/http-api/error-handling).

{% hint style="info" %}
**24/7 agents.** Track `expiresAt` and re-login proactively - a minute or two before expiry - rather than waiting for a `401`. Each re-login needs a fresh nonce and a new signature, so the bot's signing key must remain available for the lifetime of the process. Since re-login is a wallet signature (not an on-chain transaction), it costs no gas.
{% endhint %}

## Get authentication nonce

> Returns a cryptographically random nonce for use in Sign-In with Ethereum\
> (ERC-4361) authentication. The nonce is single-use and expires after 5 minutes.\
> Include this nonce in your SIWE message when authenticating.<br>

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"dreamDEX","version":"0.1.0"},"tags":[{"description":"Authentication endpoints for Sign-In with Ethereum (ERC-4361).","name":"Auth"},{"description":"Endpoint is unscoped to the account.","name":"Public"}],"servers":[{"description":"Mainnet (production)","url":"https://api.dreamdex.io"},{"description":"Testnet (staging)","url":"https://stg.api.dreamdex.io"}],"security":[],"paths":{"/v0/auth/nonce":{"get":{"description":"Returns a cryptographically random nonce for use in Sign-In with Ethereum\n(ERC-4361) authentication. The nonce is single-use and expires after 5 minutes.\nInclude this nonce in your SIWE message when authenticating.\n","operationId":"GetAuthNonce","responses":{"200":{"$ref":"#/components/responses/AuthNonce"}},"summary":"Get authentication nonce","tags":["Auth","Public"]}}},"components":{"responses":{"AuthNonce":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"properties":{"nonce":{"description":"Cryptographically random nonce for SIWE message","type":"string"}},"required":["nonce"],"type":"object"}}},"description":"Authentication nonce for SIWE"}}}}
```

## Authenticate with SIWE

> Authenticates a user by verifying a signed ERC-4361 (Sign-In with Ethereum)\
> message. The message must include a valid nonce obtained from the /auth/nonce\
> endpoint. Returns a JWT bearer token for authenticating subsequent requests.\
> The token should be included in the Authorization header as "Bearer \<token>".<br>

```json

```


# Market Data

These public endpoints return information about available trading pairs, supported currencies, and current order book depth. No [authentication](/developers/http-api/authentication) is required.

## Markets and currency codes

Endpoints that take a `{symbol}` path parameter expect a `BASE:QUOTE` pair using the exact currency codes below. The quote is always `USDso`. Note the casing and that the native Somnia token is `SOMI` (not `SOM`) - copy-pasting a placeholder such as `SOM:USD` returns [`market_not_found`](/developers/http-api/error-handling).

| Environment                | Symbols                                                  | Currency codes                            |
| -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- |
| Mainnet (`5031`)           | `SOMI:USDso`, `USDC.e:USDso`, `WBTC:USDso`, `WETH:USDso` | `SOMI`, `USDso`, `USDC.e`, `WBTC`, `WETH` |
| Testnet (Shannon, `50312`) | `SOMI:USDso`, `WBTC:USDso`, `WETH:USDso`                 | `SOMI`, `USDso`, `WBTC`, `WETH`           |

`GET /v0/markets` (below) and `GET /v0/currencies` are the canonical, always-current source of truth - query them at runtime rather than hard-coding this list. See also [Live Markets](/developers/contracts/contract-specifications#live-markets) for the corresponding contract addresses.

## List markets

> Returns all trading pairs (markets) available on this exchange. Each market\
> includes its symbol identifier along with base and quote currency information.\
> Use this endpoint to discover tradeable pairs and to populate market selection\
> interfaces. Markets are identified by symbols in the format "BASE:QUOTE"\
> (e.g., "SOMI:USDso").<br>

```json

```

## List currencies

> Returns all currencies (tokens and assets) supported by this exchange.\
> Each currency includes its identifier, ticker code, and display name.\
> Use this endpoint to discover which assets are available for trading\
> and to populate currency selection interfaces.<br>

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"dreamDEX","version":"0.1.0"},"tags":[{"name":"Exchange"},{"description":"Endpoint is unscoped to the account.","name":"Public"}],"servers":[{"description":"Mainnet (production)","url":"https://api.dreamdex.io"},{"description":"Testnet (staging)","url":"https://stg.api.dreamdex.io"}],"security":[],"paths":{"/v0/currencies":{"get":{"description":"Returns all currencies (tokens and assets) supported by this exchange.\nEach currency includes its identifier, ticker code, and display name.\nUse this endpoint to discover which assets are available for trading\nand to populate currency selection interfaces.\n","operationId":"GetCurrencies","responses":{"200":{"$ref":"#/components/responses/Currencies"}},"summary":"List currencies","tags":["Exchange","Public"]}}},"components":{"responses":{"Currencies":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"properties":{"currencies":{"items":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/CurrencyInfo"},"type":"array"}},"required":["currencies"],"type":"object"}}},"description":"List of currencies"}},"schemas":{"CurrencyInfo":{"description":"Currency information","properties":{"code":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/BaseCurrency"},"decimals":{"description":"Number of decimal places for the token (e.g., 18 for ETH, 6 for USDC)","type":"integer"},"id":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Address"},"name":{"description":"Currency display name","type":"string"}},"required":["id","code","name","decimals"],"type":"object"},"BaseCurrency":{"description":"Base currency","type":"string"},"Address":{"description":"EVM-compatible address (0x-prefixed, checksummed)","pattern":"^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$","type":"string"}}}}
```

### Order book depth

Order book depth is served **only** by the query-parameter form `GET /v0/orderbooks?symbols=<symbol>`. There is no per-market path - `GET /v0/markets/{symbol}/orderbook` and `GET /v0/orderbooks/{symbol}` both **404**.

```sh
# One market
curl "$BASE_URL/orderbooks?symbols=SOMI:USDso"
```

{% hint style="warning" %}
**Pass multiple symbols as repeated keys, not a comma-separated list.** The `symbols` parameter uses OpenAPI `style: form, explode: true`, so each symbol is its own `symbols=` key:

```sh
# Correct — repeated keys
curl "$BASE_URL/orderbooks?symbols=SOMI:USDso&symbols=WETH:USDso"

# Wrong — parsed as one literal symbol "SOMI:USDso,WETH:USDso", which matches
# no market and is silently skipped, so the response is []
curl "$BASE_URL/orderbooks?symbols=SOMI:USDso,WETH:USDso"
```

Unknown or malformed symbols are silently skipped rather than erroring, so a serialization mismatch shows up as an empty `[]` rather than a 4xx. Most HTTP clients serialize an array parameter this way automatically (e.g. `axios`/`requests` with `params={"symbols": ["A", "B"]}`); if you build the query string by hand, repeat the key.
{% endhint %}

## Get order books

> Returns aggregated order book data for one or more market symbols.\
> The response includes all open bids and asks sorted by price, with\
> quantities at each price level. Use the optional depth parameter to\
> limit the number of price levels returned. Order books are essential\
> for displaying market depth charts and calculating slippage estimates.\
> \
> If \*\*any\*\* requested symbol is not a known market, the whole request\
> is rejected with \`400 invalid\_param\` and the offending symbols are\
> listed in the error's \`context.invalid\_symbols\` array so the client\
> can correct their input. Fail-fast — clients see typos immediately\
> rather than getting a shorter list back with no way to tell why.<br>

The same repeated-key serialization applies to every array-valued `symbols` parameter in this API (`/v0/tickers`, `/v0/orderbooks`).

For per-market ticker and candle data see [Trading](/developers/http-api/trading). The endpoint below returns 24-hour OHLCV statistics for one or more markets in a single call - omit `symbols` to cover every available market; unknown symbols are silently skipped.

## Get tickers

> Returns 24-hour OHLCV market statistics for one or more markets.\
> If no symbols are specified, returns tickers for all available markets.\
> \
> When symbols are supplied and any of them is not a known market, the\
> whole request is rejected with \`400 invalid\_param\` and the offending\
> symbols are listed in the error's \`context.invalid\_symbols\` array.\
> Fail-fast — clients see typos immediately rather than getting a\
> shorter list back with no way to tell why. The all-markets path (no\
> symbols filter) has no input to validate and never returns 400.<br>


Aggregate trading volume over a half-open time range `[since, until)`, returned in both scaled and raw token units for the base and quote so volume aggregators need no client-side decimal scaling. `until` defaults to 30 minutes ago and `since` to `until - 7 days`; the range must not exceed 40 days, and an `until` newer than 30 minutes ago is rejected to allow for ingestion latency.

## Get market trading volume

> Returns the total trading volume for a market over a half-open time\
> range \`\[since, until)\`. Volume aggregates every fill in the window\
> across all traders. Both scaled (human-readable) and raw (atomic token\
> units) figures are returned for the base and quote tokens, so callers\
> such as volume aggregators need no client-side decimal scaling. Resolve\
> token addresses and decimals via \`GET /v0/markets\`.\
> \
> \`until\` defaults to 30 minutes ago when omitted; \`since\` defaults to\
> \`until - 7 days\`. The range must not exceed 40 days — longer ranges\
> return 400.\
> \
> To account for ingestion latency, an explicit \`until\` newer than 30\
> minutes ago is rejected with 400; recently executed trades may not yet\
> be queryable.<br>

# Trading

These endpoints provide access to trade history, ticker snapshots, and candlestick data for a given market. The [Order Management](#order-management) section covers preparing, querying, reducing, and cancelling orders, and [Trade History](#trade-history) returns your executed fills.

Note that the prepare-order endpoint returns an **unsigned transaction** — the client must sign and broadcast it to the Somnia network. See the [Quick Start](/developers/quick-start) guide for a full walkthrough.

{% hint style="warning" %}
**Taker orders need a crossing limit price.** A marketable (IOC/FOK) order only fills against resting liquidity priced at or through its limit: a buy must price at or above the best ask, a sell at or below the best bid. A price of `0` (raw `priceRaw = 0`) never crosses the book and produces no fill. Set a limit that crosses, bounded by your slippage tolerance.
{% endhint %}

## List recent trades

> Returns the most recent executed trades for a specific market. Each trade\
> includes the price, quantity, side (buy/sell), and timestamp. This data\
> is useful for displaying trade history feeds and analyzing recent market\
> activity. Trades are returned newest-first (reverse chronological order).\
> \
> The endpoint is public, but callers presenting a bearer token with the\
> \`trades:read\_any\` scope additionally receive the \`maker\` and \`taker\`\
> wallet addresses on each trade.<br>

```json

```

## Get ticker

> Returns 24-hour OHLCV market statistics for a specific trading pair.\
> Includes open, high, low, close prices and total trade volume.\
> All fields are "0" if no trades occurred in the period.<br>


## Get OHLCV candles

> Returns historical OHLCV (Open, High, Low, Close, Volume) candlestick data\
> for charting purposes. Each candle represents price action over a specific\
> time interval. This endpoint supports various timeframes and is essential\
> for rendering price charts and performing technical analysis.\
> \
> To page backwards through history, supply \`endTime\` as a unix millisecond\
> timestamp. Only candles whose bucket timestamp is strictly less than\
> \`endTime\` are returned. Combined with \`limit\`, this lets a client load\
> successive older windows by passing the oldest returned timestamp as the\
> next request's \`endTime\`.<br>


## Order Management

## Prepare order

> Generates an unsigned EVM transaction for placing a new order on-chain.\
> The response contains the transaction payload (to, data, value, chainId)\
> that must be signed and broadcast by the client's wallet. This non-custodial\
> approach ensures the server never handles private keys. Supports both\
> market orders (immediate execution at best price) and limit orders\
> (execution at specified price or better).\
> \
> For conditional stop orders, see \`POST /v0/markets/{symbol}/stop-orders\`.\
> \
> \### Validation rules\
> \
> A request that fails these checks returns \`400\` with a field-specific\
> \`name\` (also mirrored in the \`Error-Name\` header):\
> \
> \- \`price\` (limit orders) must be greater than zero and an exact multiple\
> &#x20; of the market's \`tickSize\`, else \`invalid\_price\`.\
> \- \`amount\` must be an exact multiple of the market's \`lotSize\` and at\
> &#x20; least its \`minQuantity\`, else \`invalid\_amount\`. There is no minimum\
> &#x20; order value (notional).\
> \- A required field omitted from the body returns \`missing\_field\`; a body\
> &#x20; that is malformed or has a field of the wrong type returns\
> &#x20; \`invalid\_request\_body\`.\
> \
> \### Spot and perpetual markets\
> \
> The same route places orders on both market kinds — a perp pool inherits\
> the identical \`placeOrder(...)\` entry point, so the request body shape is\
> unchanged. What differs is the funding source, which must match the\
> symbol's \`kind\`:\
> \
> \| Market \`kind\` | Valid \`fundingSource\` |\
> \|---|---|\
> \| \`spot\` | \`wallet\` (default), \`vault\` |\
> \| \`perp\` | \`marginBank\` |\
> \
> A mismatch is rejected with \`400 invalid\_param\`. On a perp market the\
> pool locks initial margin from the account's free MarginBank collateral\
> at placement, so fund it first via \`POST /v0/account/margin/deposit\`;\
> there is no per-order ERC-20 approval on the perp path, and the\
> \`approval\` response field is never present.\
> \
> \### Token approval for wallet funding source\
> \
> When using \`fundingSource: "wallet"\`, the SpotPool contract must have\
> sufficient ERC-20 allowance to pull tokens from the caller's wallet.\
> The server checks the caller's current on-chain allowance and only\
> includes an \`approval\` field in the response when additional approval\
> is needed. If \`approval\` is absent, the existing allowance already\
> covers the order and the caller can submit the order transaction\
> directly.\
> \
> When \`approval\` is present, the caller must submit a\
> \`token.approve(spotPoolAddress, amount)\` transaction \*\*before\*\* sending\
> the order transaction. For \*\*buy\*\* orders, approve the \*\*quote\*\* token\
> (you spend quote to buy base). For \*\*sell\*\* orders, approve the \*\*base\*\*\
> token. Use \`POST /v0/markets/{symbol}/vault/approve\` to generate the\
> approve transaction. The \`approval.amount\` covers the order cost plus\
> taker fee overhead from ceiling rounding.\
> \
> \*\*Note:\*\* Market buy orders with \`fundingSource: "wallet"\` are priced\
> off the live best ask plus \`slippageBps\` (default 5%), which bounds the\
> quote pulled from the wallet. They return a 400 error only when the ask\
> side is empty (no liquidity to price against). Fills execute at resting\
> maker prices, so \`slippageBps\` only widens how deep the order sweeps and\
> the approval headroom, not the price paid.\
> \
> \### Recommended workflow\
> \
> 1\. \*\*Simulate before submitting.\*\* Call the returned transaction via\
> &#x20;  \`eth\_call\` before sending it. The \`placeOrder\` function returns\
> &#x20;  \`(bool success, uint256 orderId)\`. If \`success\` is \`false\`, the\
> &#x20;  order will be rejected on-chain -- do not submit the transaction.\
> 2\. \*\*Sign and broadcast.\*\* If the simulation returns \`success = true\`,\
> &#x20;  sign the transaction and send it.\
> 3\. \*\*Verify after confirmation.\*\* Once the transaction confirms, check\
> &#x20;  the receipt logs for the \`OrderPlaced\` event. If the logs array is\
> &#x20;  empty, the order was silently rejected.\
> \
> \### Why an order can be rejected\
> \
> A \`placeOrder\` transaction can succeed (receipt \`status = 1\`) yet not\
> actually place an order. When this happens the call returns\
> \`(false, 0)\` and emits no events. This occurs when the order passes\
> basic validation (price, quantity, lot size) but is rejected for a\
> runtime reason such as:\
> \
> \- The order expired before execution\
> \- It would match against the sender's own resting order (self-trade)\
> \- A PostOnly order would have filled immediately\
> \- A FillOrKill order could not be completely filled\
> \- An ImmediateOrCancel order found nothing to fill against<br>

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"dreamDEX","version":"0.1.0"},"tags":[{"name":"Trading"},{"name":"Orders"},{"description":"Endpoint is scoped to the authenticated account.","name":"Private"}],"servers":[{"description":"Mainnet (production)","url":"https://api.dreamdex.io"},{"description":"Testnet (staging)","url":"https://stg.api.dreamdex.io"}],"security":[{"bearerAuth":["account"]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"bearerAuth":{"description":"Bearer auth","scheme":"bearer","type":"http"}},"parameters":{"PathSymbol":{"description":"Market symbol","in":"path","name":"symbol","required":true,"schema":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Symbol"}}},"schemas":{"Symbol":{"description":"Market symbol in `BASE:QUOTE` form using the exchange's currency codes\n(e.g. `SOMI:USDso`). There is no minimum\n  order value (notional).\n- A required field omitted from the body returns `missing_field`; a body\n  that is malformed or has a field of the wrong type returns\n  `invalid_request_body`.\n\n### Spot and perpetual markets\n\nThe same route places orders on both market kinds — a perp pool inherits\nthe identical `placeOrder(...)` entry point, so the request body shape is\nunchanged. What differs is the funding source, which must match the\nsymbol's `kind`:\n\n| Market `kind` | Valid `fundingSource` |\n|---|---|\n| `spot` | `wallet` (default), `vault` |\n| `perp` | `marginBank` |\n\nA mismatch is rejected with `400 invalid_param`. On a perp market the\npool locks initial margin from the account's free MarginBank collateral\nat placement, so fund it first via `POST /v0/account/margin/deposit`;\nthere is no per-order ERC-20 approval on the perp path, and the\n`approval` response field is never present.\n\n### Token approval for wallet funding source\n\nWhen using `fundingSource: \"wallet\"`, the SpotPool contract must have\nsufficient ERC-20 allowance to pull tokens from the caller's wallet.\nThe server checks the caller's current on-chain allowance and only\nincludes an `approval` field in the response when additional approval\nis needed. If `approval` is absent, the existing allowance already\ncovers the order and the caller can submit the order transaction\ndirectly.\n\nWhen `approval` is present, the caller must submit a\n`token.approve(spotPoolAddress, amount)` transaction **before** sending\nthe order transaction. For **buy** orders, approve the **quote** token\n(you spend quote to buy base). For **sell** orders, approve the **base**\ntoken. Use `POST /v0/markets/{symbol}/vault/approve` to generate the\napprove transaction. The `approval.amount` covers the order cost plus\ntaker fee overhead from ceiling rounding.\n\n**Note:** Market buy orders with `fundingSource: \"wallet\"` are priced\noff the live best ask plus `slippageBps` (default 5%), which bounds the\nquote pulled from the wallet. They return a 400 error only when the ask\nside is empty (no liquidity to price against). Fills execute at resting\nmaker prices, so `slippageBps` only widens how deep the order sweeps and\nthe approval headroom, not the price paid.\n\n### Recommended workflow\n\n1. **Simulate before submitting.** Call the returned transaction via\n   `eth_call` before sending it. The `placeOrder` function returns\n   `(bool success, uint256 orderId)`. If `success` is `false`, the\n   order will be rejected on-chain -- do not submit the transaction.\n2. **Sign and broadcast.** If the simulation returns `success = true`,\n   sign the transaction and send it.\n3. **Verify after confirmation.** Once the transaction confirms, check\n   the receipt logs for the `OrderPlaced` event. If the logs array is\n   empty, the order was silently rejected.\n\n### Why an order can be rejected\n\nA `placeOrder` transaction can succeed (receipt `status = 1`) yet not\nactually place an order. When this happens the call returns\n`(false, 0)` and emits no events. This occurs when the order passes\nbasic validation (price, quantity, lot size) but is rejected for a\nruntime reason such as:\n\n- The order expired before execution\n- It would match against the sender's own resting order (self-trade)\n- A PostOnly order would have filled immediately\n- A FillOrKill order could not be completely filled\n- An ImmediateOrCancel order found nothing to fill against\n","operationId":"PrepareOrder","parameters":[{"$ref":"#/components/parameters/PathSymbol"}],"requestBody":{"$ref":"#/components/requestBodies/PrepareOrder"},"responses":{"200":{"$ref":"#/components/responses/OrderTransaction"},"400":{"$ref":"#/components/responses/Error"}},"summary":"Prepare order","tags":["Trading","Orders","Private","Restricted"]}}}}
```

## Get order

> Returns detailed information about a specific order by its unique identifier.\
> The response includes the order's current status, original parameters,\
> fill progress (filled vs remaining amounts), and the on-chain transaction\
> hash if available. Use this endpoint to poll for order status updates\
> or display order details.<br>

A resting order can be shrunk without cancelling it. `newQuantityRemaining` must be at or below the current remaining quantity, at least `minQuantity`, and a multiple of `lotSize`; the order's `fullQuantity` and `quantityRemaining` both decrease by the difference. Like placing an order, this returns an unsigned transaction to sign and broadcast.

## Reduce order

> Prepares a transaction to reduce an open order to a specified remaining\
> quantity. The newQuantityRemaining must be less than or equal to the\
> current remaining quantity, at least minQuantity, and a multiple of\
> lotSize. The order's fullQuantity and quantityRemaining are both\
> decreased by the difference.\
> \
> Works on both spot and perpetual markets: a perp pool inherits the same\
> \`reduceOrder(orderId, newQuantityRemaining)\` entry point, so the request\
> and response shapes are identical. On a perp market the freed amount is\
> margin released back to the account's free MarginBank collateral.<br>

## Prepare order cancellation

> Generates an unsigned EVM transaction for canceling an open order.\
> Only orders with status "open" can be canceled. The response contains\
> the transaction payload (to, data, value, chainId) that must be signed\
> and broadcast by the client's wallet. Once confirmed on-chain, the\
> order is canceled and any unfilled portion is returned to the vault.\
> \
> Works on both spot and perpetual markets: a perp pool inherits the same\
> \`cancelOrder(orderId)\` entry point, so the request and response shapes\
> are identical. On a perp market the released amount is margin returned\
> to the account's free MarginBank collateral rather than to a spot vault.<br>

## List orders

> Returns orders for the authenticated account in a specific market,\
> newest-first. Orders can be filtered by status (open, closed, canceled,\
> expired, rejected) using the optional status query parameter. Each\
> order includes its current state, fill progress, and associated\
> transaction hash. The response includes a \`nextCursor\` token when\
> more pages are available — pass it back as \`cursor\` to continue.<br>

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"dreamDEX","version":"0.1.0"},"tags":[{"name":"Trading"},{"name":"Orders"},{"description":"Endpoint is scoped to the authenticated account.","name":"Private"}],"servers":[{"description":"Mainnet (production)","url":"https://api.dreamdex.io"},{"description":"Testnet (staging)","url":"https://stg.api.dreamdex.io"}],"security":[{"bearerAuth":["account"]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"bearerAuth":{"description":"Bearer auth","scheme":"bearer","type":"http"}},"parameters":{"PathSymbol":{"description":"Market symbol","in":"path","name":"symbol","required":true,"schema":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Symbol"}},"OrderStatus":{"description":"Order status to filter by","in":"query","name":"status","schema":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/OrderStatus"}},"OrderLimit":{"description":"Maximum number of orders to return (default 100, max 1000)","in":"query","name":"limit","schema":{"default":100,"maximum":1000,"minimum":1,"type":"integer"}},"OrderCursor":{"description":"Opaque pagination token from a prior response's `nextCursor`.\nOmit on the first request; pass the previous response's value\nunchanged to retrieve the next page. ```
```

To fetch open and historical orders across several markets in one call, use the unscoped endpoint below. Omit `symbols` to cover every available market; unknown symbols are silently skipped.

## List all orders

> Returns orders for the authenticated account across one or more markets,\
> newest-first. If no symbols are specified, returns orders for all\
> available markets. Unknown symbols are silently skipped. Optionally\
> filter by order status. The response includes a \`nextCursor\` token\
> when more pages are available — pass it back as \`cursor\` to continue.<br>


## Trade History

These endpoints return executed fills for the authenticated account. They include trades where the account was either the taker or the maker, returned newest-first. The market-scoped `trades/mine` and the unscoped `/v0/trades` (which spans several markets in one call and tags each trade with its symbol) operate on the caller's own account. The `trades/{address}` variant returns fills for any wallet and is restricted to bearer tokens carrying the `trades:read_any` scope.

## List my trades

> Returns the authenticated user's executed trades for a specific market.\
> Includes trades where the user was either the taker or the maker. Each\
> trade includes the price, quantity, side, and timestamp. Trades are\
> returned newest-first (reverse chronological order).<br>

## List all my trades

> Returns the authenticated account's executed trades across one or more\
> markets, where the account was either the taker or the maker. If no\
> symbols are specified, returns trades for all available markets. Unknown\
> symbols are silently skipped. Each trade carries its market symbol so\
> results from different markets can be told apart. Trades are returned\
> newest-first (reverse chronological order). The \`limit\` bounds the total\
> number of trades returned across all markets.<br>

## List trades for a wallet (privileged)

> Returns executed trades for the given wallet address within a market\
> over a half-open time range \`\[since, until)\`. Includes trades where\
> the wallet was either the taker or the maker. Trades are returned\
> newest-first (reverse chronological order); the response is an empty\
> array when no trades match.\
> \
> Access is restricted to bearer tokens carrying the \`trades:read\_any\`\
> scope. Tokens without the scope receive 403. Because the scope is\
> required, each trade always includes the \`maker\` and \`taker\` wallet\
> addresses.\
> \
> \`until\` defaults to the current time if omitted; \`since\` defaults to\
> \`until - 7 days\`. The range must not exceed 40 days — longer ranges\
> return 400. Use \`as=maker\` or \`as=taker\` to restrict the result to\
> one side; both sides are returned by default.<br>

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"dreamDEX","version":"0.1.0"},"tags":[{"name":"Trading"},{"description":"Endpoint is scoped to the authenticated account.","name":"Private"}],"servers":[{"description":"Mainnet (production)","url":"https://api.dreamdex.io"},{"description":"Testnet (staging)","url":"https://stg.api.dreamdex.io"}],"security":[{"bearerAuth":["trades:read_any"]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"bearerAuth":{"description":"Bearer auth","scheme":"bearer","type":"http"}},"parameters":{"PathSymbol":{"description":"Market symbol","in":"path","name":"symbol","required":true,"schema":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Symbol"}},"PathAddress":{"description":"Wallet address whose trades to return","in":"path","name":"address","required":true,"schema":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Address"}},"TradeSince":{"description":"Return trades after this unix millisecond timestamp","in":"query","name":"since","schema":{"format":"int64","type":"integer"}},"TradeUntil":{"description":"Return trades strictly before this unix millisecond timestamp\n(exclusive). Omit\nto return trades where the wallet was either maker or taker.\n","in":"query","name":"as","schema":{"enum":["maker","taker"],"type":"string"}}},"schemas":{"Symbol":{"description":"Market symbol in `BASE:QUOTE` form using the exchange's currency codes\n(e.g. `SOMI:USDso`). Pass this back as the `cursor` query\nparameter on the next request to retrieve the page after this\none. The string is intentionally opaque — clients must not parse\nor modify it. ```
```


# Builder Fees

Builder codes let a front-end or integrator charge a per-fill fee on orders it submits on a user's behalf. A wallet must first **approve** a builder, authorizing it to charge up to a chosen rate; orders are then tagged with that builder via the `builder` field on [`POST /v0/markets/{symbol}/orders`](/developers/http-api/trading#post-v0-markets-symbol-orders).

Approval is keyed on the **order owner** (the signing wallet), not the submitter, and is bounded by a protocol-wide cap. Rates are expressed in `BPS_TIMES_1K` units (one basis point = 1000). The flow is:

1. Check the protocol cap with [max-fee](#get-v0-markets-symbol-builder-max-fee). A cap of `0` means builder codes are disabled for that pool.
2. [Approve](#post-v0-markets-symbol-builder-approve) the builder for a rate at or below the cap (sign and broadcast the returned transaction). Approving `0` revokes.
3. Read back the granted rate with [approval](#get-v0-markets-symbol-builder-approval); `effective` is `min(approved, protocolMaxFee)` - the rate actually enforced at order placement.

> market's pool, in BPS\_TIMES\_1K units. A value of \`0\` means builder\
> codes are disabled for the pool.<br>

## Prepare builder approval

> Generates an unsigned EVM transaction calling \`approveBuilder\` on the\
> SpotPool, granting a builder permission to charge a per-fill fee (up to\
> \`maxFeeBpsTimes1k\`) on orders the authenticated wallet submits with that\
> builder code. The transaction targets the pool and must be signed and\
> broadcast by the wallet.\
> \
> Builder approval is keyed on the \*\*order owner\*\* (the signing wallet),\
> not the order submitter. A wallet must approve a builder before it can\
> place orders tagged with that builder via the \`builder\` field on\
> \`POST /v0/markets/{symbol}/orders\`.\
> \
> Set \`maxFeeBpsTimes1k\` to \`0\` to revoke an existing approval. The\
> approval is also bounded by the protocol-wide cap\
> (\`GET /v0/markets/{symbol}/builder/max-fee\`); a value above the cap is\
> rejected on-chain.<br>

## Get builder approval

> Returns the builder approval the authenticated wallet has granted for a\
> specific builder, in BPS\_TIMES\_1K units. \`approved\` is the raw value the\
> wallet granted (which may exceed the current protocol cap); \`effective\`\
> is \`min(approved, protocolMaxFee)\` — the rate the chain actually enforces\
> at order placement.<br>

## Collecting earned fees

Builder fees are not pushed to your wallet per fill. Each charged fee is credited to the **builder's own vault balance** on the pool it was earned on (see [`BuilderFeeCharged`](/developers/contracts/events#builderfeecharged)), where it sits as a free, withdrawable balance until you claim it. Fees accrue **per pool and per token**: buy-side (bid) fills pay in the pool's **quote** token, sell-side (ask) fills in the **base** token.

To collect:

1. **Read what you've earned** with [`getWithdrawableBalance(builder, token)`](/developers/contracts/functions#getwithdrawablebalance) on the pool, or [`GET /v0/markets/{symbol}/vault/balance`](/developers/http-api/vault), for each token you've earned in. For a native-SOMI side, pass the sentinel `0x28f34DeFd2b4CB48d9eE6d89f2Be4Bc601694c00`, not `address(0)`.
2. **Withdraw it** to your wallet with [`withdraw(token, amount)`](/developers/contracts/functions#withdraw), signed by your builder wallet — or via [`POST /v0/markets/{symbol}/vault/withdraw`](/developers/http-api/vault), which returns an unsigned transaction for you to sign and broadcast. Your key never leaves your control.

{% hint style="info" %}
The builder fee is charged on top of the trade: auto-pull pulls `principal + max(makerFee, takerFee) + builderFee` from the order owner's wallet at placement, and the builder-fee slice is routed to your withdrawable balance.
{% endhint %}

## Attributing a routed swap

Multi-hop swaps through the [SpotRouter](/developers/contracts/spot-router) can be attributed too, via its `…WithBuilder` entrypoints. Two differences from a direct order matter for accounting:

* **The fee is charged once, on leg 0 only** — not once per hop. A three-leg route pays the same builder fee as a one-leg route, and it accrues on the **leg-0 pool**, in that leg's input token. Check your balance there, not on the pool the route ends at.
* **Approval is read on the leg-0 pool.** The user must have approved you on whichever pool the route *starts* at, so a builder serving arbitrary routes needs approval on every pool that can appear first.

See [Builder attribution](/developers/contracts/spot-router#builder-attribution) for the entrypoints and the `SwapAttributed` event.


# Vault

The vault is an **optional** on-chain token custody mechanism. Orders [prepared via the HTTP API](/developers/http-api/trading#post-v0-markets-symbol-orders) default to `fundingSource: "wallet"`, which pulls tokens directly from the caller's wallet **when the order is placed** (up front, not at a later fill) via the pool's [auto-pull](/developers/contracts/functions#auto-pull-and-auto-deliver) flow (needs a prior ERC-20 approval to the SpotPool contract, or `msg.value` on native pools). Wallet auto-pull supports all order types, including resting GTC / PostOnly.

To use the vault instead, deposit tokens first and then set `fundingSource: "vault"` when [placing orders](/developers/http-api/trading#post-v0-markets-symbol-orders). The vault flow is:

1. [Approve](#post-v0-markets-symbol-vault-approve) the pool contract to spend your tokens.
2. [Deposit](#post-v0-markets-symbol-vault-deposit) them into the vault.
3. [Place orders](/developers/http-api/trading#post-v0-markets-symbol-orders) funded from the vault balance.

Vault-funded orders support all order types including resting limit orders.

{% hint style="warning" %}
**Reading a native SOMI balance directly on-chain.** When you call the vault's `getWithdrawableBalance(owner, token)` contract method for native SOMI, pass the sentinel `0x28f34DeFd2b4CB48d9eE6d89f2Be4Bc601694c00` (the `NATIVE_TOKEN` constant), **not** `address(0)` - the latter returns `0` and makes funds look missing. The HTTP `vault/balance` endpoint below abstracts this away. See [`getWithdrawableBalance`](/developers/contracts/functions#getwithdrawablebalance).
{% endhint %}

## Prepare ERC-20 approval

> Generates a standard ERC-20 \`approve(spender, amount)\` transaction that\
> authorizes the pool contract to spend the given amount of a token on\
> behalf of the wallet. The transaction targets the \*\*token\*\* contract\
> (not the pool) because ERC-20 approve is called on the token itself.\
> \
> This approval is required before:\
> \- \*\*Vault deposits\*\* — the pool must be approved to transfer tokens into\
> &#x20; the vault via the deposit endpoint.\
> \- \*\*Wallet-funded orders\*\* (\`fundingSource: "wallet"\`) — the pool must\
> &#x20; be approved to pull tokens for the atomic deposit-order-withdraw flow.\
> &#x20; The order response includes an \`approval\` field with the exact token\
> &#x20; and fee-inclusive amount needed.\
> \
> For \*\*buy\*\* orders, approve the \*\*quote\*\* token (you spend quote to buy\
> base). For \*\*sell\*\* orders, approve the \*\*base\*\* token. The amount must\
> cover the order cost plus taker fee overhead from ceiling rounding.\
> \
> \*\*Native token:\*\* when the currency is the chain's native token there\
> is no ERC-20 to approve — deposits use the payable \`depositNative()\`\
> entry point. In that case the endpoint responds with HTTP 200 and a\
> body of JSON \`null\`, signaling the client to skip signing and proceed\
> directly to the deposit endpoint.<br>

## Prepare vault deposit

> Generates an unsigned EVM transaction for depositing tokens into the\
> market's vault. The caller must first approve the pool contract to spend\
> the token via the approve endpoint. The response contains the transaction\
> payload (to, data, value, chainId) that must be signed and broadcast by\
> the client's wallet.<br>


## Prepare vault withdrawal

> Generates an unsigned EVM transaction for withdrawing tokens from the\
> market's vault back to the caller's wallet. The response contains the\
> transaction payload (to, data, value, chainId) that must be signed and\
> broadcast by the client's wallet.<br>

## Get vault balances

> Returns the withdrawable balances for both the base and quote tokens in\
> a market's vault for the authenticated wallet. Use this to display\
> available funds for trading or withdrawal.<br>

# Wallets

These privileged endpoints expose balances and trading volume for an arbitrary wallet address, broken down by market. They are intended for operators and aggregators rather than individual traders - to query your own account use [Vault balance](/developers/http-api/vault#get-v0-markets-symbol-vault-balance) and [Trade History](/developers/http-api/trading#trade-history).

Access is restricted to bearer tokens carrying the `wallet:read_any` scope; tokens without it receive `403`. See [Authentication](/developers/http-api/authentication) for how to obtain a token.

[Balances](#get-v0-wallets-wallet-balance) cover both the wallet (ERC-20 or native) and vault (withdrawable) holdings of the base and quote tokens for every market. All balances are read at a single block for a consistent snapshot; pass `blockNumber` to pin the read, otherwise the current block is used and returned in the response.

## Get wallet balances

> Returns the on-chain wallet balances and vault balances for the\
> given wallet address across all markets. For each market, the\
> response includes the base and quote token balances in both the\
> wallet (ERC-20 or native balance) and the vault (withdrawable\
> balance).\
> \
> All balances are read at the same block to ensure a consistent\
> snapshot. When \`blockNumber\` is omitted, the server fetches the\
> current block and pins all reads to it. The response includes the\
> block number used.\
> \
> Any authenticated caller may read their own wallet. Reading a\
> different wallet requires the \`wallet:read\_any\` scope; without it,\
> requesting another wallet returns 403 and an unauthenticated\
> request returns 401.<br>

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"dreamDEX","version":"0.1.0"},"tags":[{"name":"Trading"},{"description":"Endpoint is scoped to the authenticated account.","name":"Private"}],"servers":[{"description":"Mainnet (production)","url":"https://api.dreamdex.io"},{"description":"Testnet (staging)","url":"https://stg.api.dreamdex.io"}],"security":[{"bearerAuth":[]},{"bearerAuth":["wallet:read_any"]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"bearerAuth":{"description":"Bearer auth","scheme":"bearer","type":"http"}},"parameters":{"PathWallet":{"description":"Wallet address to query","in":"path","name":"wallet","required":true,"schema":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Address"}},"BlockNumber":{"description":"Pin all balance reads to this block number. number at which all balances were and vault balances across all markets"},"Error":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"properties":{"context":{"description":"Additional contextual error information","type":"object"},"description":{"description":"Human-readable error description","type":"string"},"name":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/ErrorName"},"status":{"description":"HTTP Status ```
```

[Volume](#get-v0-wallets-wallet-volume) sums the wallet's base and quote turnover per market over a half-open time range `[since, until)`, counting trades where the wallet was taker or maker. `until` defaults to the current time and `since` to `until - 7 days`; the range must not exceed 40 days.

## Get wallet trading volume

> Returns the total trading volume for the given wallet address,\
> broken down by market. Volume includes trades where the wallet\
> was either the taker or the maker. Each market entry includes\
> base volume (sum of trade quantities) and quote volume (sum of\
> trade costs).\
> \
> Any authenticated caller may read their own wallet. Reading a\
> different wallet requires the \`wallet:read\_any\` scope; without it,\
> requesting another wallet returns 403 and an unauthenticated\
> request returns 401.\
> \
> \`until\` defaults to the current time if omitted; \`since\` defaults\
> to \`until - 7 days\`. The range must not exceed 40 days.<br>

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"dreamDEX","version":"0.1.0"},"tags":[{"name":"Trading"},{"description":"Endpoint is scoped to the authenticated account.","name":"Private"}],"servers":[{"description":"Mainnet (production)","url":"https://api.dreamdex.io"},{"description":"Testnet (staging)","url":"https://stg.api.dreamdex.io"}],"security":[{"bearerAuth":[]},{"bearerAuth":["wallet:read_any"]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"bearerAuth":{"description":"Bearer auth","scheme":"bearer","type":"http"}},"parameters":{"PathWallet":{"description":"Wallet address to query","in":"path","name":"wallet","required":true,"schema":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Address"}},"TradeSince":{"description":"Return trades after this unix millisecond timestamp","in":"query","name":"since","schema":{"format":"int64","type":"integer"}},"TradeUntil":{"description":"Return trades strictly before this unix millisecond timestamp\n(exclusive).
```
